A budding yeast that is homozygous recessive for <span style="color: #006c6b;">Gene K</span> cells grow in twisted, coiled shapes, resulting in a knotted or gnarled appearance.</li> <li><strong><span style="color: #9a2d6a;">Gene M</span></strong> is connected with the '<span style="color: #9a2d6a;"><i>militant</i></span>' phenotype.
